# IT — Italy sights: individual worldwide reviews

- [Worldwide v2](../../../rules.md) · [Workflow](../../future-country-workflow.md)
- Initial editorial inventory: 62 inscribed properties and 30 tentative nominations represented at property level. 27 park/landscape records include 24 named parks in the Parks.it list, Matese and Portofino (ISPRA reports pending park authorities as of March 2026), and Gennargentu landscape with unresolved implementation. The sources count parks differently; no assertion that all 27 are operational or that their categories agree. Overlapping park/property records have explicitly distinct scopes; further duplicate review needed. Vatican City proper excluded from Rome. All GPS approximate, serial components selectively covered. Current twelve peer YAML profiles checked; narrower same-type matching and all individual material facts still need independent confirmation. No perfect-ten anchors added.
